{"parentUuid":"b7766f1b-2872-48aa-a0e5-9af5a87df2e3","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"0690d804-e582-4c3b-a42c-cd8bba120812","timestamp":"2026-05-05T14:18:27.295Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"39e05e91-b6c0-424d-864c-ea000f6476b3","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"0690d804-e582-4c3b-a42c-cd8bba120812","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"a7bc9338-e420-4593-8aef-983286d25411","timestamp":"2026-05-05T14:18:36.761Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"39e05e91-b6c0-424d-864c-ea000f6476b3","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

then\n      SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=clear + autonomous_mode=true\"\n    else\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=clear + autonomous_mode=false (HH-H.3 gate; user-driven /clear → race risk)\"\n    fi\n    ;;\n  resume)\n    SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n    FIRE_REASON=\"source=resume (user invoked claude --resume)\"\n    ;;\n  startup)\n    if [ \"$AUTONOMOUS_MODE\" = \"true\" ]; then\n      SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=startup + autonomous_mode=true\"\n    else\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=startup + autonomous_mode=false (race risk with user typing)\"\n    fi\n    ;;\nesac\n\nif [ \"$SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR\" = \"true\" ]; Implements L-S11-1 (Phase 0 hook portability), L-S13-1 (producer-consumer log\n# path mismatch), and S16 success criterion #10 (drift signal D11 / D-IDENTITY extension\n# scanning for fabricated SUPERVISED-mode regression in autonomous_mode=true ONLY-mode\n# stockforge).\n#\n# Wires: Stop hook in .claude/settings.json. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + (if violations >0) one notification\n# file in human-workspace/notifications/.\n#\n# Per Q-E2 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion frequency = phase-boundary; this hook detects\n# drift continuously but does NOT auto-promote. Surface to agent who triages next turn.\n# Per Q-E3 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion priority = hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST.\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only. L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n\nemit() {\n  local code=\"$1\" file=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n  VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${code}: ${file} — ${detail}\"$'\\n'\n}\n\n# === Check 1: L-S11-1 Phase 0 portability — no python/jq/yq/pip/npm in scripts/hooks ===\n# Exempt: comments, strings, and explicit Phase-1+ stubs marked with \"# PHASE-1+\" guard comment.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    # Skip self.\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Strip comments + heredocs roughly: scan only \"executable\" lines (not # comments).\n    NON_COMMENT=\"$(grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*#' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    # Look for forbidden tooling invocations as command tokens.\n    if printf '%s' \"$NON_COMMENT\" | grep -qE '(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])(python3?|jq|yq|pip|pip3|npm|pnpm)([[:space:]]|$)'; then\n      emit \"L-S11-1-PORTABILITY\" \"$bn\" \"non-Phase-0-portable invocation (python/jq/yq/pip/npm) — Phase 0 doctrine = bash + POSIX only\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 4: L-S43b-9 printf format-string starting with `-` needs `--` sentinel ===\n# `printf '-foo'` interprets `-foo` as an option flag, not as the format string. Fix is\n# `printf -- '-foo'`. Encountered 2x during HR-3 deploy per L-S43b-9. False-positive-safe:\n# matches only lines where printf is followed by a literal quoted dash, AND the same line\n# does NOT also contain `printf --` (which means the sentinel is already present).\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD=\"$(grep -nE \"printf[[:space:]]+['\\\"]-\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"printf[[:space:]]+--\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S43b-9-PRINTF-DASH\" \"$bn\" \"printf format starts with '-' without '--' sentinel — use 'printf -- ...'\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 2: L-S13-1 producer-consumer log path mismatch (silent telemetry brick) ===\n# Heuristic: declared LOG-like variable but never used as redirect target within same file.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Extract variable names matching *LOG*|*FILE*|*OUTPUT*|*PATH* pattern that get assigned a path-looking value.\n    DECL_VARS=\"$(grep -oE '^[A-Z_]+(LOG|FILE|OUTPUT|PATH|DIR)[A-Z_]*=' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/=$//' | sort -u || true)\"\n    for v in $DECL_VARS; do\n      [ -z \"$v\" ] && continue\n      # Skip universally-known env vars that are inputs not outputs.\n      case \"$v\" in\n        PROJECT_DIR|HOOKS_DIR|CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR|HOOK_LOG|LOOP_DIR|DOGFOOD_DIR|NOTIF_DIR|EVENTS_DIR|INDEX_DIR|ARCHIVE_DIR) continue ;;\n      esac\n      # Check for \"$VAR\" or ${VAR} appearing on right side of >> or > redirect, OR as `tee $VAR`.\n      if ! grep -qE \"(>>?|tee[[:space:]]+(-a[[:space:]]+)?)[[:space:]]*[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n        # Also accept if VAR is read FROM, or is a path argument to common command (find/cd/test/ls/mkdir/cp/mv/echo/printf/...)\n        if ! grep -qE \"[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qvE \"^[A-Z_]+=\"; then\n          # Final fallback: any non-assignment line referencing the var counts as \"used\".\n          if ! grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*${v}=\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -qE \"[\\\"]?\\\\\\$\\\\{?${v}\\\\}?[\\\"]?\"; then\n            emit \"L-S13-1-ORPHAN-LOG-VAR\" \"$bn\" \"variable \\$${v} declared but never referenced after assignment\"\n          fi\n        fi\n      fi\n    done\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 3: D-IDENTITY S16 #10 — fabricated SUPERVISED-mode regression in LIVE config ===\n# Per S15-close user correction (CHARTER-tier): full autonomous is the ONLY mode; \"SUPERVISED\"\n# was a fabricated default-until-Track-7 framing that was never user-authorized. Scan LIVE\n# config files; flag any `SUPERVISED|autonomous_mode:\\s*false|until Track 7` regression.\n# Exempt historical session/checkpoint/decision/Q&A files (they record past state legitimately).\nLIVE_TARGETS=(\n  \"$PROJECT_DIR/.claude/settings.json\"\n  \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\n  \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\"\n  \"$PROJECT_DIR/CLAUDE.md\"\n  \"$PROJECT_DIR/AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md\"\n)\n# Live constitution + proposals (excluding archived/superseded versions).\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/constitution\" ]; then\n  while IFS= read -r -d '' p; do LIVE_TARGETS+=(\"$p\"); done < <(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/constitution\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 2>/dev/null)\nfi\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/proposals\" ]; then\n  while IFS= read -r -d '' p; do LIVE_TARGETS+=(\"$p\"); done < <(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/proposals\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 2>/dev/null)\nfi\n\nREGRESSION_PATTERN='SUPERVISED[[:space:]]*mode|autonomous_mode:[[:space:]]*false|until[[:space:]]+Track[[:space:]]+7|SUPERVISED-only'\nfor t in \"${LIVE_TARGETS[@]}\"; do\n  [ ! -f \"$t\" ] && continue\n  rel=\"${t#$PROJECT_DIR/}\"\n  # Scan; deny pattern matches but allow file to legitimately quote/document the rule itself.\n  if grep -qE \"$REGRESSION_PATTERN\" \"$t\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    # Allow if the line ALSO contains \"fabricated|deprecated|anti-pattern|never user-authorized\"\n    # (i.e. file is documenting the prohibition, not violating it).\n    HITS=\"$(grep -nE \"$REGRESSION_PATTERN\" \"$t\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    while IFS= read -r line; do\n      [ -z \"$line\" ] && continue\n      if printf '%s' \"$line\" | grep -qiE 'fabricated|deprecated|anti-pattern|never user-authorized|do NOT|MUST NOT|forbidden|not user-authorized'; then\n        continue\n      fi\n      emit \"D-IDENTITY-AUTONOMOUS-REGRESSION\" \"$rel\" \"live-config line matches forbidden pattern: $(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -c1-100)\"\n      break\n    done <<< \"$HITS\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === Check 5: M-S51-1 grep-on-imagined-format anti-pattern (Pattern A) ===\n# Origin: S51 T3.1 — promotion-cycle-trigger.sh greped literal `^\\*\\*Session N\\*\\*:` against\n# real `## SXX —` header format → 0 matches → silent broken hook for ~12hr.\n# Detection: literal \"Session N\" (capital-N placeholder; never a real header) appearing\n# inside any grep/sed/awk argument. Also catches the variant with escaped asterisks.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_A=\"$(grep -nE \"(grep|sed|awk)[[:space:]].*Session N([^a-zA-Z0-9]|$)\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_A\" ]; then\n      emit \"M-S51-1-IMAGINED-FORMAT\" \"$bn\" \"literal 'Session N' placeholder in grep/sed/awk pattern — verify against real current-execution.md format (L-S51-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 7: L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep anti-pattern (Pattern C) — REFINED S54 ===\n# Origin: S48d profile-template-auto-populate.sh — set -uo pipefail + trap 'exit 0' ERR\n# + bare `grep -m1 ...` returning nonzero on no-match → ERR trap fires silently → script\n# exits 0 with NO log line → invisible failure. Fix: wrap each grep with `|| true` or\n# convert to `if grep ...; then ... fi` form.\n#\n# S54 refinement (closes KI-S53-1): prior heuristic `^[[:space:]]+grep [^|]*$` only caught\n# bare-line grep WITHOUT pipes. MISSED `session-export-raw.sh:42` form\n# `VAR=\"$(grep ... | grep ... | sed ... | tail -1)\"` — command-substitution with multi-stage\n# pipeline (the EXACT shape of M-S53-1). Refined heuristic catches:\n#   Form 1 (orig): bare line `   grep ...`\n#   Form 2 (NEW): pipeline `   grep ... | other ...` (pipefail propagates first non-zero)\n#   Form 3 (NEW): command substitution `VAR=\"$(grep ...)\"` or arg-form `... $(grep ...)`\n# False-positive guards (skip):\n#   - Comment lines (^NN:#)\n#   - Conditional form `if grep|while grep|until grep|elif grep` (exit code consumed)\n#   - Already-guarded lines containing `|| true` or `|| :`\n# Trap regex relaxed from `'[^']*' ERR` to `[^#]*ERR` to also match `trap \"...\" ERR` form.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    HAS_PIPEFAIL=\"$(grep -cE 'set [^#]*pipefail' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    HAS_ERR_TRAP=\"$(grep -cE 'trap[[:space:]]+[^#]*ERR' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    if [ \"$HAS_PIPEFAIL\" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null && [ \"$HAS_ERR_TRAP\" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then\n        emit \"L-S48d-1-PIPEFAIL-BARE-GREP\" \"$bn\" \"pipefail + ERR trap + grep without '|| true' guard — silent fail risk (L-S48d-1; refined S54 to catch command-sub + pipelines)\"\n      fi\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 8: L-S53-2 unanchored positional-marker grep (NEW Pattern D) — S54 ===\n# Origin: S53 M-S53-2 — `session-export-raw.sh` Method 1 grep `'S[0-9]+[[:space:]]+NEXT'`\n# without ^ anchor matched archive prose mid-line \"S38/S42 NEXT branching gate\" →\n# returned false-positive 42 every export, silently corrupted ALL raw-session filenames.\n# Detection: 2-pass — (a) find grep lines whose pattern contains routing-marker regex\n# tokens (S[0-9]+, Track [0-9]+, Session N, Session [0-9]+, ## S[0-9]+); D2-D8 = secondary checks. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n5\t# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n6\t# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n7\t# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\n8\tset -uo pipefail\n9\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n10\t\n11\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n12\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n13\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\n14\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n15\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n16\t\n17\tVIOLATIONS=0\n18\t\n19\temit() {\n20\t  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n21\t  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n22\t  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\t# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\n26\tcheck_loc() {\n27\t  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n28\t  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n29\t  local lines\n30\t  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n31\t  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n32\t  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n33\t  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n34\t    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n35\t      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n36\t    else\n37\t      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n38\t    fi\n39\t  fi\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n43\t  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\n44\tdone\n45\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n46\t  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\n47\tdone\n48\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n49\t  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\n50\tdone\n51\t\n52\t# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n53\t# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\n54\tSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n55\tfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n56\t  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n57\t  if grep -qE '(LOC|line count|bodies are.*LOC|within target)' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n58\t    if ! grep -qE 'wc -l' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n59\t      emit \"D2-SELF-ATTEST\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$s claim=LOC-within-target without-wc-l-verification\"\n60\t    fi\n61\t  fi\n62\tdone\n63\t\n64\t# === D3: Charter-tier item bundled with sub-charter-tier (silent absorption risk) ===\n65\tQA_BUNDLES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\" \\\n66\t                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\" \\\n67\t              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n68\tfor b in $QA_BUNDLES; do\n69\t  [ ! -f \"$b\" ] && continue\n70\t  CHARTER_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*CHARTER' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n71\t  SCOPE_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*(SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING|SUB)' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n72\t  [[ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || CHARTER_HIT=0\n73\t  [[ \"$SCOPE_HIT\"   =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || SCOPE_HIT=0\n74\t  if [ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" -ge 1 ] && [ \"$SCOPE_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n75\t    emit \"D3-CHARTER-MIXED-BUNDLE\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$b charter_count=$CHARTER_HIT scope_count=$SCOPE_HIT\"\n76\t  fi\n77\tdone\n78\t\n79\t# === D4: Spec-as-source butterfly (spec ↔ code mismatch) — handled by same-commit-rule.sh ===\n80\t# Lightweight check here: spec referenced but no matching code path exists.\n81\tSPECS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n82\tfor s in $SPECS; do\n83\t  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n84\t  REFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n85\t  for ref in $REFS; do\n86\t    if [ ! -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ref\" ]; then\n87\t      emit \"D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF\" \"LOW\" \"spec=$s missing_ref=$ref\"\n88\t    fi\n89\t  done\n90\tdone\n91\t\n92\t# === D5: Numeric values without source/as_of in agent-output files (I-S2 broad sweep) ===\n93\tDATA_FILES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n94\t                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/calibration\" \\\n95\t              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n96\tfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n97\t  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n98\t  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n99\t    if ! grep -qE '(source:|as[_ ]of:)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n100\t      emit \"D5-MISSING-CITATION\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n101\t    fi\n102\t  fi\n103\tdone\n104\t\n105\t# === D6: LLM-math anti-pattern words ===\n106\tfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n107\t  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n116\t  if ! grep -qiE '(bear[_ ]case|risks?|downside)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n117\t    emit \"D7-NO-BEAR-CASE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$f\"\n118\t  fi\n119\tdone\n120\t\n121\t# === D8: Confidence claims without calibration metadata ===\n122\tfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n123\t  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n124\t  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence|high confidence|medium confidence|low confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n125\t    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n126\t      emit \"D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n127\t    fi\n128\t  fi\n129\tdone\n130\t\n131\t# === D9: Runtime-path-leak into write-only learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree ===\n132\t# Track 5.5d.1 boundary discipline (D-005). Whitelist: hooks that legitimately write/admin events.\n133\tLEARNING_WRITE_HOOKS=\"component-telemetry|learning-queue-sweeper|learning-index-rebuild|drift-signals-D1-D9\"\n134\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md \\\n135\t         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md \\\n136\t         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; D2-D8 = secondary checks. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\nemit() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n}\n\n# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\ncheck_loc() {\n  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  local lines\n  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    else\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\n\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\ndone\n\n# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\nSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '(LOC|line count|bodies are.*LOC|within target)' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE 'wc -l' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D2-SELF-ATTEST\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$s claim=LOC-within-target without-wc-l-verification\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D3: Charter-tier item bundled with sub-charter-tier (silent absorption risk) ===\nQA_BUNDLES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor b in $QA_BUNDLES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  REFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n  for ref in $REFS; do\n    if [ ! -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ref\" ]; then\n      emit \"D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF\" \"LOW\" \"spec=$s missing_ref=$ref\"\n    fi\n  done\ndone\n\n# === D5: Numeric values without source/as_of in agent-output files (I-S2 broad sweep) ===\nDATA_FILES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/calibration\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE '(source:|as[_ ]of:)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D5-MISSING-CITATION\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D6: LLM-math anti-pattern words ===\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '(approximately|roughly|around|circa)\\s+[0-9]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D6-LLM-MATH\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D7: Bear case missing in thesis ===\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/*.md; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence|high confidence|medium confidence|low confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D9: Runtime-path-leak into write-only learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree ===\n# Track 5.5d.1 boundary discipline (D-005). -f \"$TRANSCRIPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compute running context size from transcript JSONL usage fields; fallback to file size / 3.5.\nTOTAL=$(node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs');\nconst path=process.argv[1];\nfunction pickUsage(o){return o&&(o.message&&o.message.usage)||o.usage||o.response&&o.response.usage||null;}\ntry{\n  const raw=fs.readFileSync(path,'utf8');\n  const lines=raw.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  let latest=null;\n  for(const line of lines){\n    try{const o=JSON.parse(line);const u=pickUsage(o);\n      if(u&&(u.input_tokens||u.cache_read_input_tokens||u.cache_creation_input_tokens))latest=u;\n    }catch{}\n  }\n  let ctx=0;\n  if(latest)ctx=(latest.input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_creation_input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_read_input_tokens||0);\n  if(ctx===0){const stat=fs.statSync(path);ctx=Math.floor(stat.size/3.5);}\n  console.log(ctx);\n}catch{console.log(0);}\" \"$TRANSCRIPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$TOTAL\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\n\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nprintf '[%s] watchdog tokens=%s wind_down=%s cliff=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" \"$WIND_DOWN\" \"$CLIFF\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nWIND_DOWN_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down\"\nWIND_DOWN_FIRED=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down-fired\"\nCLIFF_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.cliff-fired\"\nFAILED_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-FAILED\"\nPRE_BLOCK_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs\"\n\n# HH-H.4 (S48m): if auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh wrote a pre-block marker\n# (stale checkpoint near threshold), skip the entire spawn path. then\n    # If marker recorded a session_id different from current, OR marker pre-dates SESSION_ID file: clear.\n    local recorded_session\n    recorded_session=$(grep -oE 'session_id=[^ ]+' \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 || true)\n    if [ -z \"$recorded_session\" ] || [ \"$recorded_session\" != \"$SESSION_ID\" ]; Pipefail-bracket pattern per L-S48d-1.\n# Stop hook (priority: late chain — AFTER qa-stale-urgent-escalator.sh).\n# Authorized by D-031 (charter ratification 2026-05-05) — agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md\n# § \"Connection to human-workspace/\" Auto-mv rule.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nQA_PEND=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nQA_ANS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv-fired-${SID}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nNOW_EPOCH=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# Idempotency: skip if already fired this session\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" \"$QA_ANS\"\n\n# Skip if pending dir absent\n[ -d \"$QA_PEND\" ] || { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Global kill switch\nif [ -f \"$KILL_SWITCH\" ]; then\n  printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: kill switch %s present; skip all mv\\n' \"$TS\" \"$KILL_SWITCH\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Iterate pending bundles\nMOVED=0\nSKIPPED=0\nDEFERRED=0\n\nset +o pipefail\nPENDING_FILES=$(find \"$QA_PEND\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name \"*.md\" 2>/dev/null | sort)\nset -o pipefail\n\n[ -z \"$PENDING_FILES\" ] && { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] && continue\n  base=$(basename \"$f\")\n\n  # Condition 1: status field starts with answered-|closed-|resolved-\n  set +o pipefail\n  STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -z \"$STATUS_LINE\" ]; then\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (no status: field in head -25)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  # Strip \"status:\" prefix + leading whitespace; lowercase compare\n  STATUS_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$STATUS_LINE\" | sed 's/^status:[[:space:]]*//' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')\n\n  case \"$STATUS_VAL\" in\n    answered-*|closed-*|resolved-*) ;;  # match → continue to condition 2\n    *)\n      SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (status not answered-/closed-/resolved-: %s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Condition 2: wait_until ISO timestamp; on failure, treat as past (allow mv)\n    set +o pipefail\n    WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    set -o pipefail\n    if [ \"$WAIT_EPOCH\" -gt \"$NOW_EPOCH\" ]; then\n      DEFERRED=$((DEFERRED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: DEFER %s (wait_until=%s > NOW)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$WAIT_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # All conditions hold → mv\n  if mv \"$f\" \"$QA_ANS/$base\" 2>>\"$LOG\"; then\n    MOVED=$((MOVED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV %s → answered/ (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV-FAIL %s (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$PENDING_FILES\"\n\n# Summary log + idempotency marker\nprintf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: session=%s moved=%d deferred=%d skipped=%d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$SID\" \"$MOVED\" \"$DEFERRED\" \"$SKIPPED\" >> \"$LOG\"\necho \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n\n# Soft-warn stderr only when non-zero mv\nif [ \"$MOVED\" -gt 0 ]; never blocks Stop chain.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSYNC_HOOK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-update.sh\"\nSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.sync-tracker-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\nLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Bail if sync-tracker-update.sh missing.\n[ ! -x \"$SYNC_HOOK\" ] && [ ! -f \"$SYNC_HOOK\" ] && exit 0\n\nFIRED=0\n\n# Event 1: new ADRs authored this session (mtime <6h).\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/decisions\"\nif [ -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ]; then\n  ADR_COUNT=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name 'D-*.md' -mmin -360 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$ADR_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || ADR_COUNT=0\n  if [ \"$ADR_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    # Cap at 3 fires to avoid runaway when bulk ADRs land.\n    [ \"$ADR_COUNT\" -gt 3 ] && ADR_COUNT=3\n    for i in $(seq 1 \"$ADR_COUNT\"); do\n      bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" SCOPE charter_match \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-adr-$i\" \"\" \"auto-detected new ADR mtime <6h\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n      FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n    done\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Event 2: HIGH-severity drift this session.\nDRIFT_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.drift-signals.log\"\nif [ -f \"$DRIFT_LOG\" ]; then\n  # Use today's date prefix to scope to today (avoid stale-bucket false positive).\n  TODAY=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  if [ -n \"$TODAY\" ]; then\n    HIGH_TODAY=\"$(grep \"^\\[${TODAY}\" \"$DRIFT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -c \"severity=HIGH\" || echo 0)\"\n    [[ \"$HIGH_TODAY\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HIGH_TODAY=0\n    if [ \"$HIGH_TODAY\" -gt 0 ]; then\n      bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-drift\" \"\" \"auto-detected $HIGH_TODAY HIGH-drift events today\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n      FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Event 3: Q&A resolved this session.\nANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nif [ -d \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" ]; then\n  QA_RECENT=\"$(find \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -mmin -360 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$QA_RECENT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || QA_RECENT=0\n  if [ \"$QA_RECENT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-qa\" \"\" \"auto-detected $QA_RECENT Q&A in answered/ <6h\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n    FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Mark session as fired (regardless of FIRED count — prevents re-entry within same session).\ntouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nif [ \"$FIRED\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$TS] sync-tracker-auto-update: fired=$FIRED session=$SESSION_ID\" >> \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":85,"startLine":1,"totalLines":85}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c2127d9-cecb-4383-b638-91f1436b526e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"39e05e91-b6c0-424d-864c-ea000f6476b3","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Prior bug (M-S52-1):\n#   awked for `^**Session N**` literal which never appears in real current-execution.md\n#   → ACTIVE_SESSION silently empty → queued-grill awk matcher (~line 75) `sess != \"\"`\n#   branch never executed → session-token fire_when entries (e.g. \"S20+\", \"S52\",\n#   \"S5 Track 7\") silently never fired. Track-based matching kept working independently.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with current-execution.md (with `## S<N>` headers)\n# + queued-grill-master.md (with fire_when containing known session-token); invoke hook;\n# assert .queued-grill-fired.log contains the matched entry id (proves session-token branch fired).\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../session-start-bootstrap.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \\\n         \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\" \\\n         \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints\"\n\n# Hook requires latest.md exists else early-exits before queued-grill scan (line ~30).\ntouch \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local source_val=\"${1:-startup}\"\n  local payload\n  payload=$(printf '{\"source\":\"%s\"}' \"$source_val\")\n  # Run hook; suppress stdout/stderr (hook emits JSON + diagnostic on stdout).\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\"\n\n# --- TC1: ACTIVE_SESSION extracts LARGEST `## S<N>` → fire_when 'S52' matches ---\nrm -f \"$LOG\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**Phase**: 3.5\n\n## S52 — latest row\n## S51 — older\nEOF\n\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\" <<EOF\n# Queued Grill — Master Index\n\n### Q-TC1 — Test entry\n\n- **fire_when**: example trigger references S52 active\n- **status**: open\nEOF\n\nrun_hook \"startup\"\n\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"Q-TC1\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected Q-TC1 fire log entry; ACTIVE_SESSION extraction broken or matcher silent\"\n  echo \"Log content:\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: ACTIVE_SESSION=S52 extracted from '## S52' header → matched fire_when 'S52'\"\n\n# --- TC2: subordinate formats (S50-pre, S49a, S49b) → ACTIVE_SESSION = S<largest-numeric> ---\nrm -f \"$LOG\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**Phase**: 3.5\n\n## S50 — main\n## S50-pre — incident\n## S49b — sweep\n## S49a — audit\nEOF\n\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\" <<EOF\n# Queued Grill — Master Index\n\n### Q-TC2-pos — Should match S50\n\n- **fire_when**: trigger when S50 active\n- **status**: open\n\n### Q-TC2-neg — Should NOT match S52 (not active)\n\n- **fire_when**: trigger when S52 active\n- **status**: open\nEOF\n\nrun_hook \"startup\"\n\nif ! grep -q \"Q-TC2-pos\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected Q-TC2-pos to fire (S50 should match)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif grep -q \"Q-TC2-neg\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: Q-TC2-neg should NOT fire (S52 not in active execution)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: subordinate formats handled — S50 active; Categorization (each grep usage):\n#   `recorded_session=$(grep ... | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 || true)` — guarded ✓\n#   `if [ -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ] && grep -qE ...; then` — `&&`-chain in `if`;\n#     exit code consumed by `if` → ERR-trap exempt per bash spec ✓\n#   `if printf ... | grep -qE ...; then` — pipeline in `if`; same ✓\n#   `if [ X = \"1\" ] && grep -q ...; then` — `&&`-chain in `if`; same ✓\n#   `HIGH_COUNT=\"$(grep -c ... || echo 0)\"` — alt-guard `|| echo 0` ✓\n# All grep usages SAFE; conservative `|| true` not applied (semantics\n# already correct). Lint refinement to recognize `if X && grep` and\n# `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nWIND_DOWN=\"${STOCKFORGE_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-${ORCH_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-180000}}\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# budget-watchdog.sh — hook-fired budget watchdog (ported from orch v2.2.0).\n# Wired as PostToolUse + Stop hook in .claude/settings.json.\n# Reads transcript JSONL, sums real token usage, auto-triggers session-self-reboot at cliff.\n#\n# Stdin (JSON from Claude Code):\n#   { \"session_id\": \"...\", \"transcript_path\": \"...\", \"cwd\": \"...\", \"hook_event_name\": \"...\", ... }\n#\n# Env (STOCKFORGE_* preferred; ORCH_* legacy fallback for migration):\n#   STOCKFORGE_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS / ORCH_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS  (default 180000; was 200000 pre D-004)\n#   STOCKFORGE_CLIFF_TOKENS     / ORCH_CLIFF_TOKENS      (default 220000; was 230000 pre D-004)\n#   STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE / ORCH_WATCHDOG_DISABLE  (=1 → skip)\n# Defaults recalibrated per D-004 (UP-07): Opus 4.7 auto-compact at 160-180K + quality\n# degradation at 250K+ argue for tighter band. See agent-workspace/memory/decisions/004-up07-context-threshold-opus47.md\nset -euo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nWIND_DOWN=\"${STOCKFORGE_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-${ORCH_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-180000}}\"\nCLIFF=\"${STOCKFORGE_CLIFF_TOKENS:-${ORCH_CLIFF_TOKENS:-220000}}\"\n\n[ \"${STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE:-${ORCH_WATCHDOG_DISABLE:-0}}\" = \"1\" ] && exit 0\n\nPAYLOAD=$(cat)\nTRANSCRIPT=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).transcript_path||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nHOOK_EVENT=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).hook_event_name||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n\nif [ -z \"${TRANSCRIPT:-}\" ] || [ ! -f \"$TRANSCRIPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compute running context size from transcript JSONL usage fields; fallback to file size / 3.5.\nTOTAL=$(node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs');\nconst path=process.argv[1];\nfunction pickUsage(o){return o&&(o.message&&o.message.usage)||o.usage||o.response&&o.response.usage||null;}\ntry{\n  const raw=fs.readFileSync(path,'utf8');\n  const lines=raw.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  let latest=null;\n  for(const line of lines){\n    try{const o=JSON.parse(line);const u=pickUsage(o);\n      if(u&&(u.input_tokens||u.cache_read_input_tokens||u.cache_creation_input_tokens))latest=u;\n    }catch{}\n  }\n  let ctx=0;\n  if(latest)ctx=(latest.input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_creation_input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_read_input_tokens||0);\n  if(ctx===0){const stat=fs.statSync(path);ctx=Math.floor(stat.size/3.5);}\n  console.log(ctx);\n}catch{console.log(0);}\" \"$TRANSCRIPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$TOTAL\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\n\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nprintf '[%s] watchdog tokens=%s wind_down=%s cliff=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" \"$WIND_DOWN\" \"$CLIFF\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nWIND_DOWN_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down\"\nWIND_DOWN_FIRED=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down-fired\"\nCLIFF_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.cliff-fired\"\nFAILED_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-FAILED\"\nPRE_BLOCK_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs\"\n\n# HH-H.4 (S48m): if auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh wrote a pre-block marker\n# (stale checkpoint near threshold), skip the entire spawn path. Marker is\n# cleared by the verifier hook once checkpoint freshness restores.\nif [ -f \"$PRE_BLOCK_MARKER\" ]; then\n  printf '[%s] watchdog: PRE-BLOCKED by auto-reboot-handoff-verify (stale checkpoint); skipping reboot spawn\\n' \\\n    \"$(date -Iseconds)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Auto-recovery on prior failed reboot: clear once-only markers so retry can fire.\n# Without this, .cliff-fired / .wind-down-fired persist after a UIPI/SendKeys failure\n# (Windows secure-desktop), silently blocking ALL future reboot retries.\nif [ -f \"$FAILED_MARKER\" ]; then\n  rm -f \"$WIND_DOWN_FIRED\" \"$CLIFF_MARK\" \"$FAILED_MARKER\"\n  printf '[%s] watchdog: prior auto-reboot FAILED — cleared once-only markers to retry\\n' \\\n    \"$(date -Iseconds)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\n# S35 D7 stale-marker auto-clear (2026-05-01): if a once-only marker is older than 1 hour\n# AND the current session_id differs from the one that fired the marker, clear it so future\n# cliff/wind-down can fire fresh in the new session. Surfaced from S34-extension where\n# `.cliff-fired` from a prior session blocked re-fire after partial reboot left no\n# .auto-reboot-FAILED marker (e.g., user manually /clear'd before failure was detected).\nSESSION_ID=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).session_id||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nclear_stale_marker() {\n  local marker=\"$1\"\n  [ ! -f \"$marker\" ] && return 0\n  # Marker mtime in epoch seconds vs now: clear if >3600s old.\n  local marker_age\n  marker_age=$(($(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)))\n  if [ \"$marker_age\" -gt 3600 ]; then\n    # If marker recorded a session_id different from current, OR marker pre-dates SESSION_ID file: clear.\n    local recorded_session\n    recorded_session=$(grep -oE 'session_id=[^ ]+' \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 || true)\n    if [ -z \"$recorded_session\" ] || [ \"$recorded_session\" != \"$SESSION_ID\" ]; then\n      rm -f \"$marker\"\n      printf '[%s] watchdog: cleared stale marker %s (age=%ss recorded_session=%s current_session=%s)\\n' \\\n        \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$marker\" \"$marker_age\" \"${recorded_session:-none}\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\nclear_stale_marker \"$CLIFF_MARK\"\nclear_stale_marker \"$WIND_DOWN_FIRED\"\nclear_stale_marker \"$WIND_DOWN_MARK\"\n\nif [ \"$TOTAL\" -ge \"$WIND_DOWN\" ] && [ \"$TOTAL\" -lt \"$CLIFF\" ]; then\n  # HH-H.5b atomic marker claim (set -o noclobber) — only ONE concurrent\n  # invocation wins the race; race-loser sees `>` fail and skips.\n  if ( set -o noclobber; printf 'wind_down_crossed_at=%s session_id=%s\\ntokens=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$TOTAL\" > \"$WIND_DOWN_MARK\" ) 2>/dev/null; then\n    printf '[%s] WIND_DOWN crossed tokens=%s — auto-reboot will fire at next Stop hook\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  fi\n\n  # Sync invocation (NOT background-detached): UIPI requires interactive desktop access\n  # inherited from TUI ancestor. `timeout 8s` bounds Stop-hook latency.\n  # HH-H.5b: write WIND_DOWN_FIRED marker via noclobber atomic claim BEFORE spawning reboot\n  # so a concurrent watchdog hook (Stop + PostToolUse race window) cannot also spawn.\n  if [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" = \"Stop\" ]; then\n    if ( set -o noclobber; printf 'wind_down_fired_at=%s session_id=%s\\ntokens=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$TOTAL\" > \"$WIND_DOWN_FIRED\" ) 2>/dev/null; then\n      printf '[%s] WIND_DOWN auto-reboot firing on Stop tokens=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n      timeout 8 bash \"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/session-self-reboot.sh\" \"continue from checkpoint\" \\\n          >> \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs/auto-wind-down-$(date +%s).log\" 2>&1 || true\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cliff: HARD auto-reboot — fires on PostToolUse or Stop. Backstop for missed wind-down.\n# HH-H.5b atomic marker claim (set -o noclobber) — TOCTOU-safe vs `[ ! -f $CLIFF_MARK ]`\n# pattern (M-S48e-1 //nneeww root cause: Stop+PostToolUse hooks both passed test+spawned).\nif [ \"$TOTAL\" -ge \"$CLIFF\" ]; then\n  if ( set -o noclobber; printf 'cliff_fired_at=%s session_id=%s\\ntokens=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$TOTAL\" > \"$CLIFF_MARK\" ) 2>/dev/null; then\n    printf '[%s] CLIFF tokens=%s — firing session-self-reboot.sh\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    timeout 8 bash \"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/session-self-reboot.sh\" \"continue from checkpoint\" \\\n        >> \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs/auto-cliff-$(date +%s).log\" 2>&1 || true\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === Mode-C premature wind-down guard (Stop-only) ===\n# Triggers when LLM ends turn citing budget pressure but real tokens are well below threshold.\n# Emits Stop-blocking decision JSON to stdout per Claude Code Stop hook spec.\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" = \"Stop\" ]; then\n  EXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\n  if [ -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ] && grep -qE '^\\*\\*autonomous_mode\\*\\*:\\s*true' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    SOFT_LIMIT=$(( WIND_DOWN - 20000 ))\n    if [ \"$TOTAL\" -lt \"$SOFT_LIMIT\" ] && [ ! -f \"$WIND_DOWN_MARK\" ]; then\n      TAIL_TXT=$(tail -n 200 \"$TRANSCRIPT\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n      if printf '%s' \"$TAIL_TXT\" | grep -qE '(approaching.{0,20}200K|give next task fresh envelope|past 150K[, ]?soft.?prep|near wind.?down|wind.?down threshold|start summarizing|write checkpoint and end turn|finish in.?flight subagents)'; then\n        printf '{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"[STOCKFORGE MODE-C GUARD] Real transcript = %s tokens (threshold = %s). Self-track inflates ~25%% over real. .wind-down marker is ABSENT. Do NOT end turn citing budget pressure — dispatch the next subagent and continue. Reference: agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md § TURN-END DISCIPLINE.\"}\\n' \\\n          \"$TOTAL\" \"$WIND_DOWN\"\n        ALERT_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.autonomous-premature-windown-alert.log\"\n        mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$ALERT_LOG\")\"\n        printf '[%s] MODE-C GUARD BLOCKED Stop. real_tokens=%s soft_limit=%s wind_down_marker=absent transcript=%s\\n' \\\n          \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" \"$SOFT_LIMIT\" \"$TRANSCRIPT\" >> \"$ALERT_LOG\"\n        exit 0\n      fi\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":6,"newStart":15,"newLines":18,"lines":[" set -euo pipefail"," trap 'exit 0' ERR"," ","+# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===","+# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file.
