Categorization:\n#   FIXED S57: D4-loop bare-grep `REFS=\"$(grep -oE ... | sort -u | head -5)\"`\n#     was unguarded — no-match returned 1 → pipefail propagated → ERR-trap\n#     `exit 0` silently aborted spec scanning. Added `|| true` end-of-pipeline.\n#     REAL bug, not alt-guard form (only one in this file).\n#   Many `if grep -qE ...; then` and `if ! grep -qiE ...; then` forms — exit\n#     consumed by `if` → ERR-trap exempt per bash spec ✓\n#   Many `VAR=\"$(grep ... | wc -l | tr -d '...' || echo 0)\"` — alt-guard\n#     `|| echo 0` end-of-pipeline ✓ (KI-S54-1 recognition)\n#   `if [ X = \"1\" ] && grep -q ...; then` (line ~202) — `&&`-chain in `if` ✓\n# Per-file Check 7 design keeps flag (alt-guards not normalized to `|| true`).\n# Lint refinement deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# drift-signals-D1-D9.sh — composite drift detector running 9 grep-based signals.\n# D1 = LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY per Q-A2 answer 2026-04-29: highest empirical firing\n# rate at S2). D2-D8 = secondary checks. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\nemit() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n}\n\n# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\ncheck_loc() {\n  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  local lines\n  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    else\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\n\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\ndone\n\n# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\nSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '(LOC|line count|bodies are.*LOC|within target)' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE 'wc -l' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D2-SELF-ATTEST\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$s claim=LOC-within-target without-wc-l-verification\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D3: Charter-tier item bundled with sub-charter-tier (silent absorption risk) ===\nQA_BUNDLES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor b in $QA_BUNDLES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$b\" ] && continue\n  CHARTER_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*CHARTER' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n  SCOPE_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*(SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING|SUB)' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n  [[ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || CHARTER_HIT=0\n  [[ \"$SCOPE_HIT\"   =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || SCOPE_HIT=0\n  if [ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" -ge 1 ] && [ \"$SCOPE_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n    emit \"D3-CHARTER-MIXED-BUNDLE\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$b charter_count=$CHARTER_HIT scope_count=$SCOPE_HIT\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D4: Spec-as-source butterfly (spec ↔ code mismatch) — handled by same-commit-rule.sh ===\n# Lightweight check here: spec referenced but no matching code path exists.\nSPECS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SPECS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  REFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n  for ref in $REFS; do\n    if [ ! -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ref\" ]; then\n      emit \"D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF\" \"LOW\" \"spec=$s missing_ref=$ref\"\n    fi\n  done\ndone\n\n# === D5: Numeric values without source/as_of in agent-output files (I-S2 broad sweep) ===\nDATA_FILES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/calibration\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE '(source:|as[_ ]of:)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D5-MISSING-CITATION\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D6: LLM-math anti-pattern words ===\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '(approximately|roughly|around|circa)\\s+[0-9]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D6-LLM-MATH\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D7: Bear case missing in thesis ===\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/*.md; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if ! grep -qiE '(bear[_ ]case|risks?|downside)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D7-NO-BEAR-CASE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$f\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D8: Confidence claims without calibration metadata ===\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence|high confidence|medium confidence|low confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D9: Runtime-path-leak into write-only learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree ===\n# Track 5.5d.1 boundary discipline (D-005). Whitelist: hooks that legitimately write/admin events.\nLEARNING_WRITE_HOOKS=\"component-telemetry|learning-queue-sweeper|learning-index-rebuild|drift-signals-D1-D9\"\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE 'learning-data/(events|archive)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f category=read-path refs-write-only-tree\"\n  fi\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/scripts/hooks/*.sh; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$f\" .sh)\"\n  if printf '%s' \"$bn\" | grep -qE \"^($LEARNING_WRITE_HOOKS)$\"; then continue; fi\n  if grep -qE 'learning-data/(events|archive)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f category=non-whitelist-hook refs-write-only-tree\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === DR1 (HH-B.4 extension): Domain layer imports framework (HIGH) ===\n# Doctrine-codified per agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md DR1.\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR1_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \"from fastapi\\|from pydantic\\|from sqlalchemy\\|import psycopg\\|from redis\" \\\n    \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR1_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR1_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR1_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR1-DOMAIN-FRAMEWORK\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR1_HITS scope=packages/domain/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR3 (HH-B.4 extension): LLM call without retry/budget wrapper (MEDIUM) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/infrastructure\" ]; then\n  DR3_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \"anthropic\\.Anthropic\\|client\\.messages\\.create\" \\\n    \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/infrastructure/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -v \"with_budget\\|with_retry\\|budget_aware\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR3_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR3_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR3_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR3-LLM-NO-RETRY\" \"MEDIUM\" \"count=$DR3_HITS scope=packages/infrastructure/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR6 (HH-B.4 extension): Any type in domain package (HIGH) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR6_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \": Any\\|cast(Any\\|-> Any\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -v \"test_\\|_test\\.py\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR6_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR6_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR6_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR6-DOMAIN-ANY-TYPE\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR6_HITS scope=packages/domain/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR8 (HH-B.4 extension): Cross-BC direct import (HIGH) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR8_TOTAL=0\n  for BC in market_data fundamental company_intelligence macro news influence crowd analysis portfolio; do\n    [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/$BC\" ] || continue\n    bc_hits=\"$(grep -rn \"from packages\\.domain\\.\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/$BC/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n      | grep -v \"packages/domain/$BC/\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n    [[ \"$bc_hits\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && DR8_TOTAL=$(( DR8_TOTAL + bc_hits ))\n  done\n  if [ \"$DR8_TOTAL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR8-CROSS-BC-IMPORT\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR8_TOTAL scope=packages/domain/<BC>/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$TS] drift-signals-D1-D9: $VIOLATIONS violation(s) — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\n# Exit code: always 0 unless STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + any HIGH violation.\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ] && grep -q 'severity=HIGH' \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  HIGH_COUNT=\"$(grep -c \"severity=HIGH\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  if [ \"$HIGH_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    printf '[drift-signals] BLOCKING: %d HIGH-severity drift(s) detected with STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":8,"oldLines":6,"newStart":8,"newLines":20,"lines":[" set -uo pipefail"," trap 'exit 0' ERR"," ","+# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 retrofit (1 real fix + KI-S54-1 ratify) ===","+# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. D2-D8 = secondary checks. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\n# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 retrofit (1 real fix + KI-S54-1 ratify) ===\n# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Categorization:\n#   FIXED S57: D4-loop bare-grep `REFS=\"$(grep -oE ... | sort -u | head -5)\"`\n#     was unguarded — no-match returned 1 → pipefail propagated → ERR-trap\n#     `exit 0` silently aborted spec scanning. Added `|| true` end-of-pipeline.\n#     REAL bug, not alt-guard form (only one in this file).\n#   Many `if grep -qE ...; then` and `if ! grep -qiE ...; then` forms — exit\n#     consumed by `if` → ERR-trap exempt per bash spec ✓\n#   Many `VAR=\"$(grep ... | wc -l | tr -d '...' || echo 0)\"` — alt-guard\n#     `|| echo 0` end-of-pipeline ✓ (KI-S54-1 recognition)\n#   `if [ X = \"1\" ] && grep -q ...; then` (line ~202) — `&&`-chain in `if` ✓\n# Per-file Check 7 design keeps flag (alt-guards not normalized to `|| true`).\n# Lint refinement deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\nemit() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n}\n\n# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\ncheck_loc() {\n  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  local lines\n  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    else\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\n\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\ndone\n\n# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\nSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '(LOC|line count|bodies are.*LOC|within target)' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE 'wc -l' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D2-SELF-ATTEST\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$s claim=LOC-within-target without-wc-l-verification\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D3: Charter-tier item bundled with sub-charter-tier (silent absorption risk) ===\nQA_BUNDLES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor b in $QA_BUNDLES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$b\" ] && continue\n  CHARTER_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*CHARTER' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n  SCOPE_HIT=$(grep -cE 'level:\\s*(SCOPE|DECISION_ROUTING|SUB)' \"$b\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n  [[ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || CHARTER_HIT=0\n  [[ \"$SCOPE_HIT\"   =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || SCOPE_HIT=0\n  if [ \"$CHARTER_HIT\" -ge 1 ] && [ \"$SCOPE_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n    emit \"D3-CHARTER-MIXED-BUNDLE\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$b charter_count=$CHARTER_HIT scope_count=$SCOPE_HIT\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D4: Spec-as-source butterfly (spec ↔ code mismatch) — handled by same-commit-rule.sh ===\n# Lightweight check here: spec referenced but no matching code path exists.\nSPECS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SPECS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  REFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n  for ref in $REFS; do\n    if [ ! -f \"$PROJECT_DIR/$ref\" ]; then\n      emit \"D4-SPEC-DANGLING-REF\" \"LOW\" \"spec=$s missing_ref=$ref\"\n    fi\n  done\ndone\n\n# === D5: Numeric values without source/as_of in agent-output files (I-S2 broad sweep) ===\nDATA_FILES=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\" \\\n                   \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/calibration\" \\\n              -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '[0-9]{2,}\\.?[0-9]*\\s*(%|VND|tỷ|billion)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE '(source:|as[_ ]of:)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D5-MISSING-CITATION\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D6: LLM-math anti-pattern words ===\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '(approximately|roughly|around|circa)\\s+[0-9]' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D6-LLM-MATH\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D7: Bear case missing in thesis ===\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/*.md; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if ! grep -qiE '(bear[_ ]case|risks?|downside)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D7-NO-BEAR-CASE\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$f\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D8: Confidence claims without calibration metadata ===\nfor f in $DATA_FILES; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qiE '[0-9]+%\\s*confidence|high confidence|medium confidence|low confidence' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qiE '(n_samples|hit_rate|lookback)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D8-CONFIDENCE-NO-CALIB\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === D9: Runtime-path-leak into write-only learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree ===\n# Track 5.5d.1 boundary discipline (D-005). Whitelist: hooks that legitimately write/admin events.\nLEARNING_WRITE_HOOKS=\"component-telemetry|learning-queue-sweeper|learning-index-rebuild|drift-signals-D1-D9\"\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE 'learning-data/(events|archive)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f category=read-path refs-write-only-tree\"\n  fi\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/scripts/hooks/*.sh; do\n  [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$f\" .sh)\"\n  if printf '%s' \"$bn\" | grep -qE \"^($LEARNING_WRITE_HOOKS)$\"; then continue; fi\n  if grep -qE 'learning-data/(events|archive)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    emit \"D9-LEARNING-PATH-LEAK\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$f category=non-whitelist-hook refs-write-only-tree\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# === DR1 (HH-B.4 extension): Domain layer imports framework (HIGH) ===\n# Doctrine-codified per agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md DR1.\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR1_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \"from fastapi\\|from pydantic\\|from sqlalchemy\\|import psycopg\\|from redis\" \\\n    \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR1_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR1_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR1_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR1-DOMAIN-FRAMEWORK\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR1_HITS scope=packages/domain/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR3 (HH-B.4 extension): LLM call without retry/budget wrapper (MEDIUM) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/infrastructure\" ]; then\n  DR3_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \"anthropic\\.Anthropic\\|client\\.messages\\.create\" \\\n    \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/infrastructure/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -v \"with_budget\\|with_retry\\|budget_aware\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR3_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR3_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR3_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR3-LLM-NO-RETRY\" \"MEDIUM\" \"count=$DR3_HITS scope=packages/infrastructure/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR6 (HH-B.4 extension): Any type in domain package (HIGH) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR6_HITS=\"$(grep -rn \": Any\\|cast(Any\\|-> Any\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -v \"test_\\|_test\\.py\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$DR6_HITS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || DR6_HITS=0\n  if [ \"$DR6_HITS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR6-DOMAIN-ANY-TYPE\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR6_HITS scope=packages/domain/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === DR8 (HH-B.4 extension): Cross-BC direct import (HIGH) ===\nif [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain\" ]; then\n  DR8_TOTAL=0\n  for BC in market_data fundamental company_intelligence macro news influence crowd analysis portfolio; do\n    [ -d \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/$BC\" ] || continue\n    bc_hits=\"$(grep -rn \"from packages\\.domain\\.\" \"$PROJECT_DIR/packages/domain/$BC/\" --include=\"*.py\" 2>/dev/null \\\n      | grep -v \"packages/domain/$BC/\" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n    [[ \"$bc_hits\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && DR8_TOTAL=$(( DR8_TOTAL + bc_hits ))\n  done\n  if [ \"$DR8_TOTAL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    emit \"DR8-CROSS-BC-IMPORT\" \"HIGH\" \"count=$DR8_TOTAL scope=packages/domain/<BC>/**\"\n  fi\nfi\n\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[$TS] drift-signals-D1-D9: $VIOLATIONS violation(s) — see $LOG\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\n# Exit code: always 0 unless STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1 + any HIGH violation.\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT:-0}\" = \"1\" ] && grep -q 'severity=HIGH' \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  HIGH_COUNT=\"$(grep -c \"severity=HIGH\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  if [ \"$HIGH_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    printf '[drift-signals] BLOCKING: %d HIGH-severity drift(s) detected with STOCKFORGE_DRIFT_STRICT=1\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\" >&2\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":95,"oldLines":7,"newStart":95,"newLines":7,"lines":[" SPECS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/specs\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\""," for s in $SPECS; Scans hooks declared in\n# .claude/settings.json against firing evidence in .session-hooks.log + per-hook\n# .{hook}.log files. Flags hooks with 0 firings in the last 7 days.\n#\n# Origin: L-S51-1 empirical-firing discipline + post-mortem 2026-05-05 § HH-* gap classes.\n#   Pre-S51 fix, promotion-cycle-trigger.sh fired 20× / day yet escalated 0× because the\n#   grep pattern never matched real format. Visible firing count != effective firing.\n#   This hook catches the WEAKER signal: hooks with NO firing entries at all.\n#\n# Conformance:\n#   - Bash + POSIX per L-S11-1 (no python/jq); .claude/settings.json parsed via grep+sed\n#   - Soft-warn only (exit 0); surfaces silent count via stderr\n#   - L-S48d-1 disciplined: every grep wrapped with || true (no bare grep under pipefail+ERR-trap)\n#   - L-S48m-1 disciplined: no $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID markers\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nHOOKS_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nSETTINGS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/.claude/settings.json\"\nCOUNTER_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.hook-firing-counter.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\"\n\n# Pre-checks\n[ -f \"$SETTINGS\" ] || exit 0\n[ -f \"$HOOKS_LOG\" ] || exit 0\n\n# 7-day cutoff\nNOW_S=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\ncase \"$NOW_S\" in\n  ''|*[!0-9]*) exit 0 ;;\nesac\nCUTOFF=$(( NOW_S - 7 * 86400 ))\n\n# Extract declared hook .sh basenames from settings.json.\n# Note: cannot match `\"command\": \"...\"` value pairs directly because settings.json escapes\n# inner quotes (\\\"...\\\") which truncates the simple \"[^\"]+\" regex. Just grep for .sh\n# basenames anywhere in the settings file — every hook reference includes one.\nDECLARED=\"$(grep -oE '[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.sh' \"$SETTINGS\" 2>/dev/null \\\n            | sort -u 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n\n[ -z \"$DECLARED\" ] && exit 0\n\nDECLARED_TOTAL=0\nSILENT_COUNT=0\nSILENT_LIST=\"\"\n\nfor hook_name in $DECLARED; do\n  DECLARED_TOTAL=$(( DECLARED_TOTAL + 1 ))\n  base=\"${hook_name%.sh}\"\n\n  # Check 1: per-hook .log file mtime within 7d → recent firing\n  log_file=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.${base}.log\"\n  if [ -f \"$log_file\" ]; then\n    mtime=\"$(stat -c '%Y' \"$log_file\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f '%m' \"$log_file\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    case \"$mtime\" in\n      ''|*[!0-9]*) mtime=0 ;;\n    esac\n    if [ \"$mtime\" -gt \"$CUTOFF\" ]; then\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # Check 2: hook basename appears in session-hooks.log (any 7-day entry).\n  # Heuristic: grep -c \"$base\" — fixed-string-ish matching via -F to avoid regex pitfalls.\n  hits=\"$(grep -F -c \"$base\" \"$HOOKS_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  case \"$hits\" in\n    ''|*[!0-9]*) hits=0 ;;\n  esac\n  if [ \"$hits\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  # No firing evidence → flag\n  SILENT_COUNT=$(( SILENT_COUNT + 1 ))\n  SILENT_LIST=\"${SILENT_LIST}  - ${hook_name}\n\"\ndone\n\n# Emit\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$COUNTER_LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\nif [ \"$SILENT_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  {\n    printf '[%s] hook-firing-counter: %d/%d declared hook(s) silent ≥7d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$SILENT_COUNT\" \"$DECLARED_TOTAL\"\n    printf -- '%s' \"$SILENT_LIST\"\n  } >> \"$COUNTER_LOG\"\n  printf 'hook-firing-counter: %d hook(s) with 0 firings in last 7d — investigate per L-S49b-2 5-step playbook; detail: %s\\n' \\\n    \"$SILENT_COUNT\" \"$COUNTER_LOG\" >&2\nelse\n  printf '[%s] hook-firing-counter: all %d declared hooks fired within 7d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$DECLARED_TOTAL\" >> \"$COUNTER_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":19,"oldLines":6,"newStart":19,"newLines":15,"lines":[" set -uo pipefail"," trap 'exit 0' ERR"," ","+# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===","+# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Lint refinement\n# to recognize pipefail-bracket + `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# qa-pending-auto-mover.sh — HH-E.2 companion deliverable (Phase 2.5 harness hardening).\n#\n# Stop hook scans human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/ for bundles whose frontmatter\n# `status:` starts with `answered-|closed-|resolved-` AND whose `wait_until:` (if\n# present) ISO-8601 timestamp is in the past, AND no global `.auto-mv-paused` kill\n# switch exists. Mv qualifying bundles to human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/.\n# Idempotent per session via marker file.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Pipefail-bracket pattern per L-S48d-1.\n# Stop hook (priority: late chain — AFTER qa-stale-urgent-escalator.sh).\n# Authorized by D-031 (charter ratification 2026-05-05) — agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md\n# § \"Connection to human-workspace/\" Auto-mv rule.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nQA_PEND=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nQA_ANS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv-fired-${SID}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nNOW_EPOCH=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# Idempotency: skip if already fired this session\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" \"$QA_ANS\"\n\n# Skip if pending dir absent\n[ -d \"$QA_PEND\" ] || { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Global kill switch\nif [ -f \"$KILL_SWITCH\" ]; then\n  printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: kill switch %s present; skip all mv\\n' \"$TS\" \"$KILL_SWITCH\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Iterate pending bundles\nMOVED=0\nSKIPPED=0\nDEFERRED=0\n\nset +o pipefail\nPENDING_FILES=$(find \"$QA_PEND\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name \"*.md\" 2>/dev/null | sort)\nset -o pipefail\n\n[ -z \"$PENDING_FILES\" ] && { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] && continue\n  base=$(basename \"$f\")\n\n  # Condition 1: status field starts with answered-|closed-|resolved-\n  set +o pipefail\n  STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -z \"$STATUS_LINE\" ]; then\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (no status: field in head -25)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  # Strip \"status:\" prefix + leading whitespace; lowercase compare\n  STATUS_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$STATUS_LINE\" | sed 's/^status:[[:space:]]*//' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')\n\n  case \"$STATUS_VAL\" in\n    answered-*|closed-*|resolved-*) ;;  # match → continue to condition 2\n    *)\n      SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (status not answered-/closed-/resolved-: %s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Condition 2: wait_until ISO timestamp; if present AND > NOW → defer\n  set +o pipefail\n  WAIT_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^wait_until:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -n \"$WAIT_LINE\" ]; then\n    WAIT_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$WAIT_LINE\" | sed 's/^wait_until:[[:space:]]*//' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//')\n    # Parse ISO via date -d if available; on failure, treat as past (allow mv)\n    set +o pipefail\n    WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    set -o pipefail\n    if [ \"$WAIT_EPOCH\" -gt \"$NOW_EPOCH\" ]; then\n      DEFERRED=$((DEFERRED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: DEFER %s (wait_until=%s > NOW)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$WAIT_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # All conditions hold → mv\n  if mv \"$f\" \"$QA_ANS/$base\" 2>>\"$LOG\"; then\n    MOVED=$((MOVED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV %s → answered/ (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV-FAIL %s (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$PENDING_FILES\"\n\n# Summary log + idempotency marker\nprintf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: session=%s moved=%d deferred=%d skipped=%d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$SID\" \"$MOVED\" \"$DEFERRED\" \"$SKIPPED\" >> \"$LOG\"\necho \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n\n# Soft-warn stderr only when non-zero mv\nif [ \"$MOVED\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"qa-pending-auto-mover: auto-mv'd $MOVED bundle(s) pending/ → answered/ (deferred=$DEFERRED skipped=$SKIPPED)\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":6,"newStart":15,"newLines":16,"lines":[" set -uo pipefail"," trap 'exit 0' ERR"," ","+# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===","+# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Lint refinement","+# to recognize pipefail-bracket + `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.","+"," PROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\""," QA_PEND=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\""," QA_ANS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"505a4bfc-1ccd-4fe3-a4fc-bd7a30fb6a90","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"39e05e91-b6c0-424d-864c-ea000f6476b3","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Idempotent per session via marker file.\n#\n# Events detected:\n#   1. New ADR authored this session (mtime <6h on agent-workspace/memory/decisions/*.md)\n#      → SCOPE charter_match (+0.2 per ADR, capped at 3 to avoid runaway)\n#   2. never blocks Stop chain.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSYNC_HOOK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/sync-tracker-update.sh\"\nSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.sync-tracker-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\nLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Bail if sync-tracker-update.sh missing.\n[ ! -x \"$SYNC_HOOK\" ] && [ ! -f \"$SYNC_HOOK\" ] && exit 0\n\nFIRED=0\n\n# Event 1: new ADRs authored this session (mtime <6h).\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/decisions\"\nif [ -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ]; then\n  ADR_COUNT=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name 'D-*.md' -mmin -360 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$ADR_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || ADR_COUNT=0\n  if [ \"$ADR_COUNT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    # Cap at 3 fires to avoid runaway when bulk ADRs land.\n    [ \"$ADR_COUNT\" -gt 3 ] && ADR_COUNT=3\n    for i in $(seq 1 \"$ADR_COUNT\"); do\n      bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" SCOPE charter_match \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-adr-$i\" \"\" \"auto-detected new ADR mtime <6h\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n      FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n    done\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Event 2: HIGH-severity drift this session.\nDRIFT_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.drift-signals.log\"\nif [ -f \"$DRIFT_LOG\" ]; then\n  # Use today's date prefix to scope to today (avoid stale-bucket false positive).\n  TODAY=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  if [ -n \"$TODAY\" ]; then\n    HIGH_TODAY=\"$(grep \"^\\[${TODAY}\" \"$DRIFT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null | grep -c \"severity=HIGH\" || echo 0)\"\n    [[ \"$HIGH_TODAY\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || HIGH_TODAY=0\n    if [ \"$HIGH_TODAY\" -gt 0 ]; then\n      bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" DECISION_ROUTING drift_signal \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-drift\" \"\" \"auto-detected $HIGH_TODAY HIGH-drift events today\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n      FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Event 3: Q&A resolved this session.\nANSWERED_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nif [ -d \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" ]; then\n  QA_RECENT=\"$(find \"$ANSWERED_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -mmin -360 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]' || echo 0)\"\n  [[ \"$QA_RECENT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || QA_RECENT=0\n  if [ \"$QA_RECENT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    bash \"$SYNC_HOOK\" DECISION_ROUTING q_and_a_resolution \"\" \"auto-S${SESSION_ID:0:8}-qa\" \"\" \"auto-detected $QA_RECENT Q&A in answered/ <6h\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n    FIRED=$(( FIRED + 1 ))\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Mark session as fired (regardless of FIRED count — prevents re-entry within same session).\ntouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nif [ \"$FIRED\" -gt 0 ]; Real production behavior at S56 close: ACTIVE_TRACK=\"Track 5\" because\nS53-row narrative (line ~107 of `current-execution.md`) mentioned \"Track 5\" mid-prose for\nauto-mover production-smoke evidence — false-positive.\n\n### Categorization (per L-S56-1 3-class typology)\n- File target: `current-execution.md` → markdown-with-prose\n- Content shape: free-form prose in S<N> session rows\n- Class: **B (free-form-prose-in-markdown)**\n- `^` anchor advice from Check 8 → INVALID for Class B (Track refs are mid-line in prose)\n- Real fix: structural — parse bounded section FIRST, then content-search\n\n### Fix applied\n```bash\n# OLD\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\n# NEW\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null \\\n                 | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -1 || true)\n```\n\nawk range pattern `/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/` outputs ONLY the `## Active Focus Track`\nsection bounded by next `---` separator. Archive prose in older S<N> session rows lives\nOUTSIDE the section → excluded. `|| true` end-of-pipeline preserves L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap\ndiscipline.\n\n### Firing-test extension (4 → 6 TCs; cumulative session-start-bootstrap 6/6 PASS)\n| TC | Validates | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| TC1 | ACTIVE_SESSION extraction from `## S<N>` largest header | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC2 | subordinate formats (S50/S50-pre/S49b) → S<largest-numeric> | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC3 | phantom `**Session N**:` literal NOT matched | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC4 | ACTIVE_TRACK matcher works independently of ACTIVE_SESSION (UPDATED — fixture moved Track 7 into Active Focus Track section per new design) | PASS |\n| TC5 | **NEW** KI-S56-1 fix: Track ref IN Active Focus Track section MATCHES (positive) | PASS |\n| TC6 | **NEW** KI-S56-1 fix: Track ref ONLY in archive prose does NOT MATCH (negative) | PASS |\n\n### Pre-deploy iteration-bug catch (per L-S52-3 SUCCESS path of L-S51-1)\nTC4 baseline FAIL after KI-S56-1 fix shipped. Root cause: TC4 fixture (authored at S53)\nplaced `Track 7` in session-row narrative (`## S52 — Track 7 work`) — exactly the buggy\nmid-prose pattern KI-S56-1 fix eliminates. Original test inadvertently encoded the bug\nas the expected behavior. Updated fixture: Track 7 moved INTO `## Active Focus Track`\nsection. Caught at firing-test stage → SUCCESS path; **NOT catalogued as M-S57-X**.\n\n### Production smoke against real `current-execution.md` (post-fix)\n```\n$ awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md \\\n    | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -5\n(empty — Phase 3.5 uses T-prefix shorthand exclusively in Active Focus Track section)\n\n$ grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md | head -3\nTrack 7   (archive prose — would be falsely picked by old code)\nTrack 7\nTrack 5\n\n$ rm -f agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\n$ echo '{\"source\":\"startup\"}' | bash scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh\n$ cat agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\n(empty — 0 spurious queued-grill emissions)\n```\n\n**Comparison vs pre-fix**: would have emitted 9 false-positives for closed Q-* entries\n(Q-B2 + Q-C2 + Q-C3 + Q-D3 + Q-E1 + Q-E2 + Q-E3 + Q-E4 + Q-2.1) all containing \"Track 7\"\nin their fire_when. Post-fix: 0 emissions. Validated empirically.\n\n---\n\n## Deliverable #2 — L-S48d-1 sample of 5 hooks for KI-S54-1 categorization\n\nTop-5 priority candidates from S54 production-smoke 27-list. Each VBW'd → categorized →\nratify-via-comment OR real fix applied per categorization outcome.\n\n### #2.1 — budget-watchdog.sh (RATIFY-VIA-COMMENT — alt-guard form)\nCategorized 5 grep usages: 1 already-guarded `|| true`, 3 `if X && grep` or `if X | grep`\n(exit consumed by `if`, ERR-trap exempt per bash spec), 1 alt-guard `|| echo 0`. **All\nSAFE; no real bug; lint flag false-positive per KI-S54-1.**\n\nEdit: 12-line ratify comment after `trap 'exit 0' ERR` documenting categorization.\n\n### #2.2 — drift-signals-D1-D9.sh (1 REAL FIX + RATIFY-VIA-COMMENT)\n**REAL bug fixed**: line 84 D4-loop bare-grep\n```bash\n# BEFORE:\nREFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n# AFTER:\nREFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5 || true)\"\n```\n\nBug analysis: D4 spec-dangling-ref check loops through recent specs. If ANY spec has no\n`packages/.py` or `apps/.py` reference, bare-grep returns 1 → pipefail propagates →\nERR-trap `exit 0` silently aborts spec scanning. Real production behavior at S56 close: ACTIVE_TRACK=\"Track 5\" because\nS53-row narrative (line ~107 of `current-execution.md`) mentioned \"Track 5\" mid-prose for\nauto-mover production-smoke evidence — false-positive.\n\n### Categorization (per L-S56-1 3-class typology)\n- File target: `current-execution.md` → markdown-with-prose\n- Content shape: free-form prose in S<N> session rows\n- Class: **B (free-form-prose-in-markdown)**\n- `^` anchor advice from Check 8 → INVALID for Class B (Track refs are mid-line in prose)\n- Real fix: structural — parse bounded section FIRST, then content-search\n\n### Fix applied\n```bash\n# OLD\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\n# NEW\nACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null \\\n                 | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -1 || true)\n```\n\nawk range pattern `/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/` outputs ONLY the `## Active Focus Track`\nsection bounded by next `---` separator. Archive prose in older S<N> session rows lives\nOUTSIDE the section → excluded. `|| true` end-of-pipeline preserves L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap\ndiscipline.\n\n### Firing-test extension (4 → 6 TCs; cumulative session-start-bootstrap 6/6 PASS)\n| TC | Validates | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| TC1 | ACTIVE_SESSION extraction from `## S<N>` largest header | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC2 | subordinate formats (S50/S50-pre/S49b) → S<largest-numeric> | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC3 | phantom `**Session N**:` literal NOT matched | PASS (regression-clean) |\n| TC4 | ACTIVE_TRACK matcher works independently of ACTIVE_SESSION (UPDATED — fixture moved Track 7 into Active Focus Track section per new design) | PASS |\n| TC5 | **NEW** KI-S56-1 fix: Track ref IN Active Focus Track section MATCHES (positive) | PASS |\n| TC6 | **NEW** KI-S56-1 fix: Track ref ONLY in archive prose does NOT MATCH (negative) | PASS |\n\n### Pre-deploy iteration-bug catch (per L-S52-3 SUCCESS path of L-S51-1)\nTC4 baseline FAIL after KI-S56-1 fix shipped. Root cause: TC4 fixture (authored at S53)\nplaced `Track 7` in session-row narrative (`## S52 — Track 7 work`) — exactly the buggy\nmid-prose pattern KI-S56-1 fix eliminates. Original test inadvertently encoded the bug\nas the expected behavior. Updated fixture: Track 7 moved INTO `## Active Focus Track`\nsection. Caught at firing-test stage → SUCCESS path; **NOT catalogued as M-S57-X**.\n\n### Production smoke against real `current-execution.md` (post-fix)\n```\n$ awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md \\\n    | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -5\n(empty — Phase 3.5 uses T-prefix shorthand exclusively in Active Focus Track section)\n\n$ grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md | head -3\nTrack 7   (archive prose — would be falsely picked by old code)\nTrack 7\nTrack 5\n\n$ rm -f agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\n$ echo '{\"source\":\"startup\"}' | bash scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh\n$ cat agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\n(empty — 0 spurious queued-grill emissions)\n```\n\n**Comparison vs pre-fix**: would have emitted 9 false-positives for closed Q-* entries\n(Q-B2 + Q-C2 + Q-C3 + Q-D3 + Q-E1 + Q-E2 + Q-E3 + Q-E4 + Q-2.1) all containing \"Track 7\"\nin their fire_when. Each VBW'd → categorized →\nratify-via-comment OR real fix applied per categorization outcome.\n\n### #2.1 — budget-watchdog.sh (RATIFY-VIA-COMMENT — alt-guard form)\nCategorized 5 grep usages: 1 already-guarded `|| true`, 3 `if X && grep` or `if X | grep`\n(exit consumed by `if`, ERR-trap exempt per bash spec), 1 alt-guard `|| echo 0`. **All\nSAFE; no real bug; lint flag false-positive per KI-S54-1.**\n\nEdit: 12-line ratify comment after `trap 'exit 0' ERR` documenting categorization.\n\n### #2.2 — drift-signals-D1-D9.sh (1 REAL FIX + RATIFY-VIA-COMMENT)\n**REAL bug fixed**: line 84 D4-loop bare-grep\n```bash\n# BEFORE:\nREFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5)\"\n# AFTER:\nREFS=\"$(grep -oE 'packages/[a-z_/-]+\\.py|apps/[a-z_/-]+\\.py' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null | sort -u | head -5 || true)\"\n```\n\nBug analysis: D4 spec-dangling-ref check loops through recent specs. {"parentUuid":"c463d542-ddd8-4c7c-89f8-81c31c2f6e05","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"4827f3d2-c5f8-4a14-8ce9-36842cefcc7b","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RqdTAitNo9BRDPzphCMRSQ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Agent Notes — Learned Rules\n2\t\n3\t> Append-mostly. Each rule earned through real experience (post-mortem, drift detection, user correction).\n4\t> Rules here have higher status than convention but lower than constitution.\n5\t\n6\t## Rule Format\n7\t\n8\tUse this template:\n9\t\n10\t```markdown\n11\t### YYYY-MM-DD: [Short rule name]\n12\t**Context**: What situation triggered learning this\n13\t**Rule**: The actionable rule\n14\t**Anti-example**: What was done wrong\n15\t**Correct example**: What should be done instead\n16\t**Severity**: critical | high | medium | low\n17\t**Auto-detect**: yes/no — can a drift signal catch this?\n18\t```\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## Initial Rules (from charter design)\n23\t\n24\t### 2026-04-23: LLM Never Outputs Numbers\n25\t**Context**: Stock domain risk — LLM-generated numbers in finance lead to real money loss\n26\t**Rule**: LLM never returns numerical values as natural-language output. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n27\t**Anti-example**:\n28\t```\n29\tLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n30\t```\n31\t**Correct example**:\n32\t```\n33\tLLM tool calls:\n34\t  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n35\t  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n36\t  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\n37\tLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n38\t```\n39\t**Severity**: critical\n40\t**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n41\t\n42\t### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n43\t**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n44\t**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n45\t**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n46\t**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n47\t**Severity**: high\n48\t**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n49\t\n50\t### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data\n51\t**Context**: LLM \"confidence\" is qualitative; user reads numerical values\n52\t**Rule**: Any \"X% confidence\" or \"high/medium/low confidence\" claim must trace to historical hit rate from calibration database. Include `n_samples`, `lookback_period`, `hit_rate` metadata.\n53\t**Anti-example**: \"70% confidence this is a buy\"\n54\t**Correct example**: \"Confidence: MEDIUM (signal pattern: cyclical_bottom_with_dividend; historical n=23 cases since 2018; hit rate 65% over 12 months; lookback=78 months)\"\n55\t**Severity**: high\n56\t**Auto-detect**: partial — flag confidence claims without metadata\n57\t\n58\t### 2026-04-23: Source Provider Disagreement Surfaced\n59\t**Context**: vnstock vs FiinPro vs TCBS sometimes disagree on same data point\n60\t**Rule**: When sources disagree by >1%, output shows divergence explicitly. Never silently pick one.\n61\t**Anti-example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ\" (silent average or arbitrary pick)\n62\t**Correct example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ ± 200 tỷ (vnstock: 35,000 / FiinPro: 35,200 / TCBS: 34,800; reconciled with HIGH confidence)\"\n63\t**Severity**: medium\n64\t**Auto-detect**: no (requires source metadata in output template)\n65\t\n66\t---\n67\t\n68\t## Add New Rules Here\n69\t\n70\t### 2026-04-29: Q&A Bundle Skeleton MUST Carry `defer_cycle` Field\n71\t**Context**: While drafting `qa-escalation/references/sample-bundle.md` in S2, I initially omitted `defer_cycle` from the bundle frontmatter — caught on review against Decision 002 § Track 2 R7 mitigation.\n72\t**Rule**: Every Q&A bundle file in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/` MUST include `defer_cycle: 0` at creation, and increment on every reopening / supersession. SessionStart hook (Track 5) keys can-kicking detection off this field.\n73\t**Anti-example**: Bundle with frontmatter `id / topic / opened_at / expected_answer_by / priority / status` — passes superficial validation but R7 mitigation is invisible.\n74\t**Correct example**: Bundle frontmatter includes `defer_cycle: 0` from creation; every Decision in `related_decisions:` also has `defer_cycles` field syncronized.\n75\t**Severity**: medium\n76\t**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5 `qa-pending-stale-mover.sh` hook should reject bundles missing the field.\n77\t\n78\t### 2026-04-29: User Communication Idiom — \"ok rồi. continue\" / \"ok continue\" = Trivial Acknowledgment\n79\t**Context**: User in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_03.txt` and via chat replies repeatedly used the idiom \"ok rồi. continue\" or \"ok continue\" as the standard \"I accept the proposed defaults; proceed\" signal in Q&A round acceptance flows.\n80\t**Rule**: Treat both phrases as TRIVIAL acknowledgment in lite-detect (`user-prompt-intake/references/lite-detect-patterns.md`). Do NOT dispatch intent-classifier on these. They specifically mean \"accept all defaults of the most recent agent-proposed bundle / decision\"."}]},"uuid":"56cce41c-5622-4554-9149-63907382e72d","timestamp":"2026-05-05T14:33:37.421Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"# Agent Notes — Learned Rules\n\n> Append-mostly. All numbers come from deterministic Python functions invoked via tool use. LLM only interprets results.\n**Anti-example**:\n```\nLLM output: \"Based on my analysis, the company's ROE is approximately 18%, P/E is around 12, and intrinsic value is roughly 95,000 VND per share.\"\n```\n**Correct example**:\n```\nLLM tool calls:\n  compute_roe(net_income=2_345B, equity=13_120B) → 17.87%\n  compute_pe(price=85_000, eps=7_054) → 12.05\n  compute_intrinsic_value_dcf(...) → 94_523 VND\nLLM output: \"ROE = 17.87% (FY2024), P/E = 12.05 (TTM), DCF intrinsic value = 94,523 VND. Versus current price 85,000 VND, suggests ~11% margin of safety.\"\n```\n**Severity**: critical\n**Auto-detect**: yes — regex for \"approximately\", \"around\", \"roughly\" + numbers in LLM output\n\n### 2026-04-23: Bear Case Required for Every Thesis\n**Context**: Confirmation bias is the #1 retail investor mistake\n**Rule**: Every thesis output must include substantive bear case (≥3 distinct points, each grounded in evidence). System refuses to render thesis without it.\n**Anti-example**: Thesis with bull case + risks section that says \"market volatility, regulatory changes\" (boilerplate)\n**Correct example**: Bear case with specific points like \"Q3 inventory turnover dropped 23% YoY suggesting demand weakness\", \"Major competitor X launched directly competitive product Y in March 2026\", \"Net working capital deterioration of 15% indicates cash flow strain\"\n**Severity**: high\n**Auto-detect**: yes — bear case length + specificity check\n\n### 2026-04-23: Confidence Requires Calibration Data\n**Context**: LLM \"confidence\" is qualitative; user reads numerical values\n**Rule**: Any \"X% confidence\" or \"high/medium/low confidence\" claim must trace to historical hit rate from calibration database. Include `n_samples`, `lookback_period`, `hit_rate` metadata.\n**Anti-example**: \"70% confidence this is a buy\"\n**Correct example**: \"Confidence: MEDIUM (signal pattern: cyclical_bottom_with_dividend; historical n=23 cases since 2018; hit rate 65% over 12 months; lookback=78 months)\"\n**Severity**: high\n**Auto-detect**: partial — flag confidence claims without metadata\n\n### 2026-04-23: Source Provider Disagreement Surfaced\n**Context**: vnstock vs FiinPro vs TCBS sometimes disagree on same data point\n**Rule**: When sources disagree by >1%, output shows divergence explicitly. Never silently pick one.\n**Anti-example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ\" (silent average or arbitrary pick)\n**Correct example**: \"Revenue Q3 2024: 35,000 tỷ ± 200 tỷ (vnstock: 35,000 / FiinPro: 35,200 / TCBS: 34,800; reconciled with HIGH confidence)\"\n**Severity**: medium\n**Auto-detect**: no (requires source metadata in output template)\n\n---\n\n## Add New Rules Here\n\n### 2026-04-29: Q&A Bundle Skeleton MUST Carry `defer_cycle` Field\n**Context**: While drafting `qa-escalation/references/sample-bundle.md` in S2, I initially omitted `defer_cycle` from the bundle frontmatter — caught on review against Decision 002 § Track 2 R7 mitigation.\n**Rule**: Every Q&A bundle file in `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/` MUST include `defer_cycle: 0` at creation, and increment on every reopening / supersession. SessionStart hook (Track 5) keys can-kicking detection off this field.\n**Anti-example**: Bundle with frontmatter `id / topic / opened_at / expected_answer_by / priority / status` — passes superficial validation but R7 mitigation is invisible.\n**Correct example**: Bundle frontmatter includes `defer_cycle: 0` from creation; every Decision in `related_decisions:` also has `defer_cycles` field syncronized.\n**Severity**: medium\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Track 5 `qa-pending-stale-mover.sh` hook should reject bundles missing the field.\n\n### 2026-04-29: User Communication Idiom — \"ok rồi. continue\" / \"ok continue\" = Trivial Acknowledgment\n**Context**: User in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_03.txt` and via chat replies repeatedly used the idiom \"ok rồi. continue\" or \"ok continue\" as the standard \"I accept the proposed defaults; proceed\" signal in Q&A round acceptance flows.\n**Rule**: Treat both phrases as TRIVIAL acknowledgment in lite-detect (`user-prompt-intake/references/lite-detect-patterns.md`). Without it, file-path-target Class B cases would get wrong fix advice (anchor) when real fix is structural.\n**Auto-detect**: partial — Check 8 flags all 3 classes uniformly; triage requires LLM 2-step inspection (cheap once recipe encoded).\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S56-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/promotion-cycle-trigger.sh` + `scripts/hooks/session-start-bootstrap.sh` + `scripts/hooks/stale-prompt-detector.sh` inline comments cite L-S55-1 (which now points here for refined heuristic)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S56-batch-retrofit-3-hooks.log` § L-S55-1 categorization refined heuristic post-S56\n\n---\n\n### KI-S56-1 — session-start-bootstrap.sh:73 first-Track latent bug (deferred structural refactor)\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Origin**: S56 categorization step for session-start-bootstrap.sh:73 — `head -1` of `Track [0-9]+` returns the FIRST `Track <N>` reference in current-execution.md regardless of where it appears. {"parentUuid":"7791a07d-22b4-43f7-8460-b1509609a2c1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_018somwqcLwYUwmmjnPwgeTW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Mdot7bomV2qku1sGJHPtJU","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S56-batch-retrofit-3-hooks.log` § KI-S56-1\n","new_string":"- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S56-batch-retrofit-3-hooks.log` § KI-S56-1\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S57-KI-S56-1-fix-and-L-S48d-1-sample.log` § KI-S56-1 → CLOSED\n\n---\n\n### L-S57-1 — Class B (free-form-prose-in-markdown) structural-refactor recipe codified\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Context**: KI-S56-1 fix at S57 — operationalizing L-S56-1 Class B doctrine (\"real fix is structural — parse section/header bounds first\") into a concrete reusable recipe. Prior to S57, Class B doctrine existed but each application would hand-design the section boundary parser. Codification removes that repetition.\n\n**Rule (CONCRETE recipe for Class B fixes)**: when a Check 8 flag categorizes as Class B (file-target + free-form-prose content; e.g. Track refs inline in markdown narrative), apply the structural-refactor recipe:\n\n```bash\n# Pattern: parse bounded structural section FIRST via awk range, THEN content-search.\nACTIVE_VAL=$(awk '/^## Section Header/,/^---/' \"$FILE\" 2>/dev/null \\\n               | grep -oE 'pattern' | head -1 || true)\n```\n\nComponents:\n1. **Section anchor**: `^## <Header>` or equivalent stable structural marker (NOT inline prose).\n2. **Section closer**: `^---` (markdown horizontal rule between sections), or `^## ` (next sibling header), or end-of-file.\n3. **Content extractor**: ordinary `grep -oE '<pattern>'` on the bounded output.\n4. **Pipefail discipline**: `|| true` end-of-pipeline preserves L-S48d-1 ERR-trap exemption.\n\n**Validation (REQUIRED)**: 2 firing-test cases per fix:\n- **Positive (TC-N)**: target value IN section → MATCHES. Fixture: file with `## Section Header` containing pattern + closing `---`.\n- **Negative (TC-(N+1))**: target value ONLY in archive-prose / outside section → does NOT match. Fixture: file with target pattern in S<X>-row narrative BEFORE `## Section Header`, AND `## Section Header` content has no pattern. Asserts ACTIVE_VAL=\"\" and downstream consumer's `val != \"\"` guard short-circuits.\n\n**Anti-example** (KI-S56-1 prior bug): unbounded `grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' file | head -1` returned first `Track <N>` reference anywhere in file → archive-prose false-positive.\n\n**Correct example** (KI-S56-1 S57 fix): `awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' file | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -1 || true` — bounded to Active Focus Track section only.\n\n**Severity**: medium — generalizable for any future Class B fix; Codification removes that repetition.\n\n**Rule (CONCRETE recipe for Class B fixes)**: when a Check 8 flag categorizes as Class B (file-target + free-form-prose content; e.g. Track refs inline in markdown narrative), apply the structural-refactor recipe:\n\n```bash\n# Pattern: parse bounded structural section FIRST via awk range, THEN content-search.\nACTIVE_VAL=$(awk '/^## Section Header/,/^---/' \"$FILE\" 2>/dev/null \\\n               | grep -oE 'pattern' | head -1 || true)\n```\n\nComponents:\n1. **Section anchor**: `^## <Header>` or equivalent stable structural marker (NOT inline prose).\n2. **Section closer**: `^---` (markdown horizontal rule between sections), or `^## ` (next sibling header), or end-of-file.\n3. **Content extractor**: ordinary `grep -oE '<pattern>'` on the bounded output.\n4. **Pipefail discipline**: `|| true` end-of-pipeline preserves L-S48d-1 ERR-trap exemption.\n\n**Validation (REQUIRED)**: 2 firing-test cases per fix:\n- **Positive (TC-N)**: target value IN section → MATCHES.
